Both render the existing logs page so clicking the nav\n+      # items never errors; the real pages land in the `trace-map-leitstand`\n+      # and `war-room-dedicated-page` stories, which will repoint these routes.\n+      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive, :index\n+      live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive, :index\n     end\n   end\n end\ndiff --git a/server/priv/static/assets/app.js b/server/priv/static/assets/app.js\nindex acd2e22b..ff8a6d88 100644\n--- a/server/priv/static/assets/app.js\n+++ b/server/priv/static/assets/app.js\n@@ -43,6 +43,51 @@\n     );\n   }\n \n+  // admin-sidebar-nesting: expand/collapse for the nested sidebar nav sections\n+  // (\"Logs\", \"Configuration\" — Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1 parent mode).\n+  //\n+  // The admin CSP is `script-src 'self'` (no 'unsafe-inline'), and the sidebar\n+  // renders on BOTH dead controller pages (no LiveView socket — a `phx-click`\n+  // would never fire) and LiveView pages, so the toggle lives here as a single\n+  // DELEGATED document-level click listener (survives LiveView DOM patches;\n+  // nothing to re-wire after morphdom). A native <button> fires `click` for\n+  // mouse, Space, and Enter alike, so this one listener is fully\n+  // keyboard-operable (story AC9).\n+  //\n+  // Toggling flips three things the server rendered (expanded by default —\n+  // story Dev Notes Q3; collapse state intentionally does NOT persist across\n+  // navigations, Q1):\n+  //   - aria-expanded on the <button data-nav-toggle> (screen readers)\n+  //   - the boolean `hidden` attribute on the aria-controls'd child <ul>\n+  //     (children stay in the DOM — AC10; the <ul> carries no display utility\n+  //     class, so the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }` actually applies —\n+  //     see the trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 flex-beats-hidden bug)\n+  //   - the chevron's `rotate-90` class (visual open/closed state — AC13)\n+  function wireNavSectionToggles() {\n+    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n+      var target = event.target;\n+      if (!target || typeof target.closest !== \"function\") return;\n+      var button = target.closest(\"button[data-nav-toggle]\");\n+      if (!button) return;\n+\n+      var nowExpanded = button.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") !== \"true\";\n+      button.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", nowExpanded ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n+\n+      var listId = button.getAttribute(\"aria-controls\");\n+      var list = listId ? document.getElementById(listId) : null;\n+      if (list) {\n+        if (nowExpanded) {\n+          list.removeAttribute(\"hidden\");\n+        } else {\n+          list.setAttribute(\"hidden\", \"hidden\");\n+        }\n+      }\n+\n+      var icon = button.querySelector('[data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]');\n+      if (icon) icon.classList.toggle(\"rotate-90\", nowExpanded);\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n   // admin-add-user-autocomplete: CSP-safe fetch typeahead for the room/space add-member\n   // field. The admin CSP is `script-src 'self'` (no 'unsafe-inline') but same-origin\n   // fetch IS allowed, and these pages are dead controller renders (no LiveView socket).\n@@ -1130,6 +1175,7 @@\n \n   function boot() {\n     wireConfirmForms();\n+    wireNavSectionToggles();\n     wireUserAutocomplete();\n     wireTraceMapFilters();\n     wireDetailPanelFocus();","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"319f2b0b-45be-40ea-995f-4564148ff763","timestamp":"2026-07-07T11:08:32.092Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"diff --git a/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex b/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex\nindex f2b08f3d..4a7c4575 100644\n--- a/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex\n+++ b/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex\n@@ -219,6 +219,13 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.Router do\n       # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream +\n       # Trace Map, subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic.\n       live \"/logs\", LogsLive, :index\n+      # enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting (AC7 stub guidance): the nested\n+      # \"Logs\" nav section links to these two pages before they exist as their\n+      # own views. A native <button> fires `click` for\n+  // mouse, Space, and Enter alike, so this one listener is fully\n+  // keyboard-operable (story AC9).\n+  //\n+  // Toggling flips three things the server rendered (expanded by default —\n+  // story Dev Notes Q3; collapse state intentionally does NOT persist across\n+  // navigations, Q1):\n+  //   - aria-expanded on the <button data-nav-toggle> (screen readers)\n+  //   - the boolean `hidden` attribute on the aria-controls'd child <ul>\n+  //     (children stay in the DOM — AC10; the <ul> carries no display utility\n+  //     class, so the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }` actually applies —\n+  //     see the trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 flex-beats-hidden bug)\n+  //   - the chevron's `rotate-90` class (visual open/closed state — AC13)\n+  function wireNavSectionToggles() {\n+    document.addEventListener(\"click\", function (event) {\n+      var target = event.target;\n+      if (!target || typeof target.closest !== \"function\") return;\n+      var button = target.closest(\"button[data-nav-toggle]\");\n+      if (!button) return;\n+\n+      var nowExpanded = button.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") !== \"true\";\n+      button.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", nowExpanded ? \"true\" : \"false\");\n+\n+      var listId = button.getAttribute(\"aria-controls\");\n+      var list = listId ? document.getElementById(listId) : null;\n+      if (list) {\n+        if (nowExpanded) {\n+          list.removeAttribute(\"hidden\");\n+        } else {\n+          list.setAttribute(\"hidden\", \"hidden\");\n+        }\n+      }\n+\n+      var icon = button.querySelector('[data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]');\n+      if (icon) icon.classList.toggle(\"rotate-90\", nowExpanded);\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n   // admin-add-user-autocomplete: CSP-safe fetch typeahead for the room/space add-member\n   // field. {"parentUuid":"64010607-59f1-44db-8bdc-6c1cf8425da9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"90d26025-2b2c-439e-8eaf-4677446dbd12","agentId":"a65e9efd17f8c0889","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LaxoJoCzY8QMgDUtCC7cXh","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LayoutsNestedNavTest do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting`\n4\t  (Acceptance Tests 1-11 and 13 from the story's \"Acceptance Tests\" section;\n5\t  Acceptance Test 12 — zero-regression of the existing Playwright/BDD E2E\n6\t  suite under the refactored nav — is a genuinely browser-only proof and\n7\t  lives in `e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature` +\n8\t  `e2e/step-definitions/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.steps.ts` instead. That\n9\t  file also carries the real-keyboard half of AC9/AC10 (Tab/Space/Enter on\n10\t  an actual focused DOM node — not reproducible against\n11\t  `Phoenix.LiveViewTest`'s server-rendered HTML string). This mirrors the\n12\t  codebase's established split between component-level ExUnit coverage and\n13\t  real-browser Playwright coverage for the sibling `trace-map-hierarchy`\n14\t  story (see `logs_live/trace_map_tree_test.exs`'s own moduledoc for the\n15\t  identical pattern, and `logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature`'s header comment\n16\t  for the browser-only half).\n17\t\n18\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting.md\n19\t\n20\t  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n21\t\n22\t  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) is\n23\t  currently a PRIVATE (`defp`) 3-attr leaf-only component with no\n24\t  `:children`/`:expanded` attr and no parent-with-toggle rendering mode at\n25\t  all. Every `render_component(&Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1, ...)` call\n26\t  below fails with `** (UndefinedFunctionError) function\n27\t  Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1 is undefined or private` — calling a private\n28\t  function via a remote capture is a compile-time-valid, runtime-failing\n29\t  reference (mirrors this codebase's own `trace_map_tree_test.exs`\n30\t  precedent for a target function that doesn't exist at all yet). The two\n31\t  integration `describe` blocks at the bottom fail for a different, equally\n32\t  real reason: `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` still renders \"Role Mapping\" /\n33\t  \"Claim Mapping\" / \"Configuration\" as three flat sibling `<li>` items and\n34\t  has no \"Logs\" parent with child stubs at all.\n35\t\n36\t  ## Component contract this file proposes (for the dev agent to implement —\n37\t  concretizes the story's Dev Notes \"File Structure\" section, which only\n38\t  sketches an example)\n39\t\n40\t  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` becomes PUBLIC and gains two new attrs on\n41\t  top of the three (`:navigate`, `:key`, `:active`) that already exist:\n42\t\n43\t    * `:children` — list of `%{navigate: String.t(), key: String.t(),\n44\t      label: String.t()}` maps, default `[]`. Empty (the default) renders\n45\t      EXACTLY today's leaf markup (`<li><a data-navkey=... aria-current=...\n46\t      class=...>label</a></li>`) — zero behavior change for every existing\n47\t      call site that does not pass `:children` (AC2's \"a leaf item ...\n48\t      renders exactly as before\"). This plain-map shape (rather than the\n49\t      Dev Notes' sketched `:child` slot) mirrors the sibling\n50\t      `TraceMapTree.endpoint_tree/1` component's own precedent of plain\n51\t      data attrs over slots for a repeated child structure — the dev agent\n52\t      may implement it with a slot instead IF the resulting HTML satisfies\n53\t      every assertion below unchanged.\n54\t    * `:expanded` — boolean, default `true` (Dev Notes Q3's recommended\n55\t      \"all expanded by default\"). This file only pins down the RENDER\n56\t      contract for a given `:expanded` value; what `live/1` computes that\n57\t      value FROM (Dev Notes item 2's `parent_is_active?/3` sketch) is the\n58\t      dev agent's wiring choice, exercised instead by the integration\n59\t      `describe` blocks below via real `active_nav` values already assigned\n60\t      by unrelated, unchanged controllers.\n61\t\n62\t  When `:children` is non-empty, `nav_item/1` renders a PARENT:\n63\t\n64\t    * A `<button type=\"button\" data-navkey={key} aria-expanded={to_string(\n65\t      expanded)} aria-controls={\"nav-\\#{key}-children\"}>` (NOT an `<a>` —\n66\t      AC9), containing a toggle-icon element (`data-testid=\n67\t      \"nav-toggle-icon\"`, AC13) followed by the slot content (the parent's\n68\t      own label).\n69\t    * Toggle-icon element carries a class that differs between expanded and\n70\t      collapsed (AC13's \"rotates/changes when expanded/collapsed\") — this\n71\t      file pins down `\"rotate-90\"` present in the icon's class list when\n72\t      `expanded: true`, absent when `expanded: false`, as the concrete,\n73\t      testable proxy for that rotation (a CSS `transition` on that class\n74\t      swap is an implementation detail this file does not test).\n75\t    * Highlight classes on that `<button>`, in priority order (AC3/AC4):\n76\t        1. `key == active` (the parent itself is the active nav) → the\n77\t           SAME primary classes leaf items use today (`bg-primary\n78\t           text-primary-content font-semibold`).\n79\t        2. `active` matches one of `children`'s `:key`s (a child is active)\n80\t           → a SECONDARY/accent class, `bg-base-300` (already used\n81\t           elsewhere in this file for hover state — AC14's \"no new color\n82\t           classes\"), explicitly NOT `bg-primary`.\n83\t        3. otherwise (active belongs to neither this parent nor any of its\n84\t           children — e.g. it names a key in a wholly different section)\n85\t           → the same neutral classes leaf items use today. This is the\n86\t           render-contract half of Acceptance Test 13.\n87\t    * A sibling `<ul id={\"nav-\\#{key}-children\"} role=\"list\" class=\"ml-4 ...\">`\n88\t      (AC12's indentation) carrying the boolean `hidden` attribute whenever\n89\t      `expanded` is `false` (AC10 — children stay in the DOM either way,\n90\t      Floki can always find them; only the `hidden` attribute toggles).\n91\t      Each child renders `<li><a navigate={child.navigate}\n92\t      data-navkey={child.key} aria-current={if child.key == active, do:\n93\t      \"page\"} class={...}>{child.label}</a></li>` — primary classes when\n94\t      `child.key == active`, neutral otherwise (AC4's \"other children ...\n95\t      neutral\").\n96\t\n97\t  `live/1` itself must additionally: (a) reorganize \"Configuration\" into a\n98\t  parent with `role-mapping`/`claim-mapping` children (AC5/AC6, removing\n99\t  the two flat top-level items), (b) introduce a new \"Logs\" parent with\n100\t  child stubs `logs-monitoring` (label \"Monitoring\", already-shipped\n101\t  `/admin/logs`), `logs-config` (label \"Configuration\", `/admin/logs/config`)\n102\t  and `logs-war-room` (label \"War Room\", `/admin/logs/war-room`) per\n103\t  AC7/AC8, PLUS — per the story's later \"Design Update (2026-07-07)\"\n104\t  addendum — a 4th child `logs-trace-map` (label \"Trace Map\",\n105\t  `/admin/logs/trace-map`, the not-yet-built page from the Leitstand spec).\n106\t  The addendum's wording (\"gains a third child 'Trace Map'\") does not\n107\t  literally square with AC7's own enumeration of 3 stubs; per this\n108\t  pipeline run's explicit instruction that \"the story is authoritative\n109\t  where it and the spec differ in scope,\" this file treats the addendum as\n110\t  ADDITIVE to AC7 (4 children total) rather than replacing any of AC7's 3\n111\t  named stubs. See the dedicated describe block below and this run's ATDD\n112\t  report for the full reasoning — a deviation worth the dev/reviewer's\n113\t  explicit attention.\n114\t\n115\t  ## Auth / rendering strategy for the two integration `describe` blocks\n116\t\n117\t  Mirrors `config_controller_test.exs`'s `build_admin_conn/2` +\n118\t  `Nebu.Admin.Endpoint.call/2` pattern EXACTLY. A full router-dispatched\n119\t  request is required here (unlike `live_isolated/3` elsewhere in this test\n120\t  suite) because `live_isolated/3` explicitly skips both the root AND the\n121\t  `:live` layout — it would never render the sidebar at all. Real GETs to\n122\t  the already-shipped `/admin/config` and `/admin/config/role-mapping`\n123\t  controller routes exercise the REAL, production `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`\n124\t  nav list and the REAL `active_nav` values `RoleMappingController` already\n125\t  assigns (`\"role-mapping\"`) — nothing about those two controllers changes\n126\t  in this story, only how the shared layout renders their (unchanged)\n127\t  `active_nav`.\n128\t  \"\"\"\n129\t\n130\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n131\t\n132\t  import Plug.Test\n133\t  import Plug.Conn\n134\t  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest, only: [render_component: 2]\n135\t\n136\t  alias Nebu.Admin.Layouts\n137\t\n138\t  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n139\t  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-admin-sidebar-nesting-test-key\", 2)\n140\t\n141\t  # ── component-level helpers (mirrors trace_map_tree_test.exs) ──────────\n142\t\n143\t  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_fragment!(html)\n144\t\n145\t  defp render_nav_item(assigns) do\n146\t    render_component(&Layouts.nav_item/1, assigns) |> parsed()\n147\t  end\n148\t\n149\t  @config_children [\n150\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/role-mapping\", key: \"role-mapping\", label: \"Role Mapping\"},\n151\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/claim-mapping\", key: \"claim-mapping\", label: \"Claim Mapping\"}\n152\t  ]\n153\t\n154\t  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n155\t  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n156\t  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n157\t\n158\t  defp class_includes?(node, class_name) do\n159\t    case attr(node, \"class\") do\n160\t      nil -> false\n161\t      class -> class |> String.split() |> Enum.member?(class_name)\n162\t    end\n163\t  end\n164\t\n165\t  defp hidden?(node), do: attr(node, \"hidden\") != nil\n166\t\n167\t  # ── integration-level helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────\n168\t\n169\t  defp build_admin_conn(method, path) do\n170\t    %{conn(method, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n171\t    |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n172\t    |> fetch_session()\n173\t    |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n174\t      \"admin\" => true,\n175\t      \"sub\" => \"admin-1\",\n176\t      \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n177\t    })\n178\t  end\n179\t\n180\t  defp endpoint_call(conn), do: @endpoint.call(conn, @endpoint.init([]))\n181\t\n182\t  defp install_bootstrap_completed do\n183\t    Application.put_env(:nebu_http, :admin_bootstrap_store, fn\n184\t      :bootstrap_completed?, [] -> {:ok, true}\n185\t      _op, _args -> :default\n186\t    end)\n187\t\n188\t    on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu_http, :admin_bootstrap_store) end)\n189\t  end\n190\t\n191\t  defp get_page_html(path) do\n192\t    conn = build_admin_conn(:get, path) |> endpoint_call()\n193\t    assert conn.status == 200, \"expected 200 rendering #{path}, got #{conn.status}\"\n194\t    Floki.parse_document!(conn.resp_body)\n195\t  end\n196\t\n197\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n198\t  # Acceptance Test 1 [AC1-AC2]\n199\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n200\t\n201\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 1 [AC1-AC2]: nested nav items render with expand/collapse toggle\" do\n202\t    test \"a parent item with children renders a toggle icon and a nested <ul> of children\" do\n203\t      html =\n204\t        render_nav_item(%{\n205\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n206\t          key: \"config\",\n207\t          active: nil,\n208\t          children: @config_children\n209\t        })\n210\t\n211\t      assert find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"])),\n212\t             \"AC1: expected a toggle/chevron icon inside the parent button\"\n213\t\n214\t      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n215\t      assert children_ul, \"AC1: expected a nested <ul id=\\\"nav-config-children\\\">\"\n216\t\n217\t      assert length(find_all(children_ul, \"li > a\")) == 2,\n218\t             \"AC1: expected exactly 2 rendered children\"\n219\t    end\n220\t\n221\t    test \"a leaf item (no children) renders exactly as before — no toggle icon, plain <a>, no <button>\" do\n222\t      html = render_nav_item(%{navigate: \"/admin/dashboard\", key: \"dashboard\", active: nil})\n223\t\n224\t      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"])),\n225\t             \"AC1: a leaf item must NOT render a toggle icon\"\n226\t\n227\t      assert find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"dashboard\"])),\n228\t             \"AC2: leaf item must render as a plain <a data-navkey=\\\"dashboard\\\">\"\n229\t\n230\t      refute find(html, \"button\"), \"AC2: leaf item must not render a <button>\"\n231\t    end\n232\t  end\n233\t\n234\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n235\t  # Acceptance Test 2 [AC3-AC4]\n236\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n237\t\n238\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 2 [AC3-AC4]: active-nav extends to parent when a child is active\" do\n239\t    test \"child active: child gets primary highlight, parent gets secondary, other children neutral\" do\n240\t      html =\n241\t        render_nav_item(%{\n242\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n243\t          key: \"config\",\n244\t          active: \"role-mapping\",\n245\t          children: @config_children\n246\t        })\n247\t\n248\t      parent_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n249\t      assert parent_button, \"expected the Configuration parent to render as a <button>\"\n250\t\n251\t      assert class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-base-300\"),\n252\t             \"AC4: parent must get the secondary/accent highlight when a child is active\"\n253\t      refute class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-primary\"),\n254\t             \"AC4: parent must NEVER get the full primary highlight, even when a child is active\"\n255\t\n256\t      role_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]))\n257\t      assert class_includes?(role_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\"),\n258\t             \"AC4: the active child must get the full primary highlight\"\n259\t      assert attr(role_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n260\t\n261\t      claim_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"claim-mapping\"]))\n262\t      refute class_includes?(claim_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\"),\n263\t             \"AC4: other (inactive) children must be neutral\"\n264\t\n265\t      # Test-review MINOR-2: aria-current=\"\" is INVALID ARIA — on an inactive\n266\t      # item the attribute must be entirely ABSENT. (Phoenix's\n267\t      # `aria-current={if cond, do: \"page\"}` idiom omits nil attribute values,\n268\t      # so `nil` is the only correct rendered outcome here.)\n269\t      assert attr(claim_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == nil,\n270\t             \"AC4: an inactive child must not carry aria-current at all (\\\"\\\" is invalid ARIA)\"\n271\t    end\n272\t  end\n273\t\n274\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n275\t  # Acceptance Test 3 [AC3]\n276\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n277\t\n278\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 3 [AC3]: parent itself active\" do\n279\t    test \"parent gets primary highlight, all children remain neutral\" do\n280\t      html =\n281\t        render_nav_item(%{\n282\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n283\t          key: \"config\",\n284\t          active: \"config\",\n285\t          children: @config_children\n286\t        })\n287\t\n288\t      parent_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n289\t      assert class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-primary\"),\n290\t             \"AC3: the parent itself must get the primary highlight when it is the active nav\"\n291\t\n292\t      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n293\t        link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"#{key}\"]))\n294\t        refute class_includes?(link, \"bg-primary\"),\n295\t               \"AC3: child #{key} must be neutral while the parent itself is active\"\n296\t      end\n297\t    end\n298\t  end\n299\t\n300\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n301\t  # Acceptance Test 6 [AC9]\n302\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n303\t\n304\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 6 [AC9]: parent items render as buttons with aria-expanded\" do\n305\t    test \"aria-expanded is \\\"true\\\" when expanded: true\" do\n306\t      html =\n307\t        render_nav_item(%{\n308\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n309\t          key: \"config\",\n310\t          active: nil,\n311\t          children: @config_children,\n312\t          expanded: true\n313\t        })\n314\t\n315\t      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n316\t      assert button, \"AC9: parent must render as a <button>, not an <a>\"\n317\t      assert attr(button, \"aria-expanded\") == \"true\"\n318\t    end\n319\t\n320\t    test \"aria-expanded is \\\"false\\\" when expanded: false\" do\n321\t      html =\n322\t        render_nav_item(%{\n323\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n324\t          key: \"config\",\n325\t          active: nil,\n326\t          children: @config_children,\n327\t          expanded: false\n328\t        })\n329\t\n330\t      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n331\t      assert attr(button, \"aria-expanded\") == \"false\"\n332\t    end\n333\t  end\n334\t\n335\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n336\t  # Acceptance Test 7 [AC9]\n337\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n338\t\n339\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 7 [AC9]: aria-controls establishes relationship to the child list\" do\n340\t    test \"button's aria-controls matches the child <ul>'s id\" do\n341\t      html =\n342\t        render_nav_item(%{\n343\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n344\t          key: \"config\",\n345\t          active: nil,\n346\t          children: @config_children\n347\t        })\n348\t\n349\t      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n350\t      assert attr(button, \"aria-controls\") == \"nav-config-children\"\n351\t\n352\t      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n353\t      assert children_ul, \"expected a <ul id=\\\"nav-config-children\\\"> matching aria-controls\"\n354\t    end\n355\t  end\n356\t\n357\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n358\t  # Acceptance Test 8 [AC10-AC11] (render-contract half — see moduledoc for\n359\t  # the real-keyboard half in the Playwright feature file)\n360\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n361\t\n362\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 8 [AC10]: collapsed children remain present in the DOM\" do\n363\t    test \"when expanded: false, child <li>/<a> elements are still found by Floki (not removed), only `hidden`\" do\n364\t      html =\n365\t        render_nav_item(%{\n366\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n367\t          key: \"config\",\n368\t          active: nil,\n369\t          children: @config_children,\n370\t          expanded: false\n371\t        })\n372\t\n373\t      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n374\t      assert hidden?(children_ul),\n375\t             \"AC10: the collapsed child list must carry the `hidden` attribute\"\n376\t\n377\t      assert find(children_ul, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"])),\n378\t             \"AC10: children must remain in the DOM (not conditionally excluded) even when collapsed\"\n379\t      assert find(children_ul, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"claim-mapping\"]))\n380\t    end\n381\t\n382\t    test \"when expanded: true, the child list does not carry `hidden`\" do\n383\t      html =\n384\t        render_nav_item(%{\n385\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n386\t          key: \"config\",\n387\t          active: nil,\n388\t          children: @config_children,\n389\t          expanded: true\n390\t        })\n391\t\n392\t      refute hidden?(find(html, \"#nav-config-children\"))\n393\t    end\n394\t  end\n395\t\n396\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n397\t  # Acceptance Test 10 [AC12]\n398\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n399\t\n400\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 10 [AC12]: nested children are visually indented\" do\n401\t    test \"the child <ul> carries the ml-4 indentation class\" do\n402\t      html =\n403\t        render_nav_item(%{\n404\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n405\t          key: \"config\",\n406\t          active: nil,\n407\t          children: @config_children\n408\t        })\n409\t\n410\t      assert class_includes?(find(html, \"#nav-config-children\"), \"ml-4\"),\n411\t             \"AC12: expected the nested child <ul> to carry ml-4 (or equivalent) indentation\"\n412\t    end\n413\t  end\n414\t\n415\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n416\t  # Acceptance Test 11 [AC13]\n417\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n418\t\n419\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 11 [AC13]: parent items show a toggle chevron that changes with state\" do\n420\t    test \"the toggle icon's class differs between expanded and collapsed\" do\n421\t      expanded_html =\n422\t        render_nav_item(%{\n423\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n424\t          key: \"config\",\n425\t          active: nil,\n426\t          children: @config_children,\n427\t          expanded: true\n428\t        })\n429\t\n430\t      collapsed_html =\n431\t        render_nav_item(%{\n432\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n433\t          key: \"config\",\n434\t          active: nil,\n435\t          children: @config_children,\n436\t          expanded: false\n437\t        })\n438\t\n439\t      expanded_icon = find(expanded_html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]))\n440\t      collapsed_icon = find(collapsed_html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]))\n441\t\n442\t      assert class_includes?(expanded_icon, \"rotate-90\"),\n443\t             \"AC13: expected the toggle icon to carry a rotation class when expanded\"\n444\t      refute class_includes?(collapsed_icon, \"rotate-90\"),\n445\t             \"AC13: expected the toggle icon's rotation class to be absent when collapsed\"\n446\t    end\n447\t  end\n448\t\n449\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n450\t  # Acceptance Test 13 [AC3-AC4] (component-level half — proves the\n451\t  # highlight-priority logic correctly falls through to \"neutral\" when\n452\t  # `active` names a key from an entirely different, unrelated section;\n453\t  # the two real top-level sections never share render state in `live/1`\n454\t  # either, so testing one `nav_item/1` instance in isolation IS the\n455\t  # correct unit boundary for this invariant)\n456\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n457\t\n458\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 13 [AC3-AC4]: parent has no highlight when active_nav belongs to a different section\" do\n459\t    test \"Configuration gets neutral classes when active_nav is \\\"logs-monitoring\\\" (a Logs child, not its own)\" do\n460\t      html =\n461\t        render_nav_item(%{\n462\t          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n463\t          key: \"config\",\n464\t          active: \"logs-monitoring\",\n465\t          children: @config_children\n466\t        })\n467\t\n468\t      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n469\t      refute class_includes?(button, \"bg-primary\"),\n470\t             \"must not be primary-highlighted for an unrelated section's active key\"\n471\t      refute class_includes?(button, \"bg-base-300\"),\n472\t             \"must not be secondary-highlighted for an unrelated section's active key\"\n473\t\n474\t      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n475\t        refute class_includes?(find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])), \"bg-primary\")\n476\t      end\n477\t    end\n478\t  end\n479\t\n480\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n481\t  # Acceptance Test 4 [AC5-AC6] — full-layout integration\n482\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n483\t\n484\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 4 [AC5-AC6]: Configuration nesting removes flat Role Mapping / Claim Mapping\" do\n485\t    setup do\n486\t      install_bootstrap_completed()\n487\t      :ok\n488\t    end\n489\t\n490\t    test \"Role Mapping / Claim Mapping are no longer top-level items, but ARE nested Configuration children\" do\n491\t      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n492\t\n493\t      # Test-review MAJOR-1: Floki 0.38.4 silently treats a LEADING child\n494\t      # combinator (`find_all(li_node, \"> a\")`) as a DESCENDANT selector,\n495\t      # which would have made this list include correctly-NESTED children and\n496\t      # fail the refutes below against a CORRECT implementation. Full-path\n497\t      # child combinators ARE honored, so select the direct top-level items in\n498\t      # one shot: only <a>/<button> elements that are DIRECT children of a\n499\t      # top-level <li> (nested children live one level deeper, inside the\n500\t      # parent's own <ul id=\"nav-*-children\"> > li > a — never matched here).\n501\t      top_level_keys =\n502\t        html\n503\t        |> find_all(\n504\t          ~s(nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > a) <>\n505\t            ~s(, nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > button)\n506\t        )\n507\t        |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n508\t\n509\t      refute \"role-mapping\" in top_level_keys,\n510\t             \"AC6: Role Mapping must be removed as a flat top-level item\"\n511\t      refute \"claim-mapping\" in top_level_keys,\n512\t             \"AC6: Claim Mapping must be removed as a flat top-level item\"\n513\t      assert \"config\" in top_level_keys,\n514\t             \"AC5: Configuration must remain as a single top-level item\"\n515\t\n516\t      config_children = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n517\t      assert config_children, \"AC5: expected a #nav-config-children nested list\"\n518\t\n519\t      nested_keys = config_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n520\t\n521\t      assert \"role-mapping\" in nested_keys,\n522\t             \"AC6: Role Mapping must be visible nested under Configuration when expanded\"\n523\t      assert \"claim-mapping\" in nested_keys,\n524\t             \"AC6: Claim Mapping must be visible nested under Configuration when expanded\"\n525\t    end\n526\t\n527\t    test \"real active_nav wiring: on /admin/config/role-mapping, Configuration is secondary-highlighted and Role Mapping is primary-highlighted\" do\n528\t      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config/role-mapping\")\n529\t\n530\t      config_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n531\t      assert config_button, \"expected Configuration to render as a parent <button>\"\n532\t      assert class_includes?(config_button, \"bg-base-300\")\n533\t      refute class_includes?(config_button, \"bg-primary\")\n534\t\n535\t      role_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]))\n536\t      assert class_includes?(role_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\")\n537\t      assert attr(role_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n538\t    end\n539\t  end\n540\t\n541\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n542\t  # Acceptance Test 5 [AC7-AC8] — full-layout integration\n543\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n544\t\n545\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 5 [AC7-AC8]: Logs section is created with child stubs\" do\n546\t    setup do\n547\t      install_bootstrap_completed()\n548\t      :ok\n549\t    end\n550\t\n551\t    test \"a Logs parent item is visible, and expanding it shows Monitoring / Configuration / War Room stubs\" do\n552\t      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n553\t\n554\t      logs_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"logs\"]))\n555\t      assert logs_button, \"AC7: expected a \\\"Logs\\\" parent item\"\n556\t\n557\t      logs_children = find(html, \"#nav-logs-children\")\n558\t      assert logs_children, \"AC7: expected a #nav-logs-children nested list\"\n559\t\n560\t      nested_keys = logs_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n561\t\n562\t      assert \"logs-monitoring\" in nested_keys,\n563\t             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"Monitoring\\\" stub with key logs-monitoring\"\n564\t      assert \"logs-config\" in nested_keys,\n565\t             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"Configuration\\\" stub with key logs-config\"\n566\t      assert \"logs-war-room\" in nested_keys,\n567\t             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"War Room\\\" stub with key logs-war-room\"\n568\t\n569\t      for key <- nested_keys do\n570\t        assert String.starts_with?(key, \"logs-\"),\n571\t               \"AC8: every Logs child key must be namespaced with \\\"logs-\\\" to avoid collisions, got #{key}\"\n572\t      end\n573\t\n574\t      # AC7: the child link actually points somewhere navigable (no dead href).\n575\t      monitoring_link = find(logs_children, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"]))\n576\t      assert attr(monitoring_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs\"\n577\t    end\n578\t  end\n579\t\n580\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n581\t  # Design Update (2026-07-07, appended to the story) — Trace Map nav item\n582\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n583\t\n584\t  describe \"Design Update: Logs section additionally includes a Trace Map child (additive to AC7's 3 stubs)\" do\n585\t    setup do\n586\t      install_bootstrap_completed()\n587\t      :ok\n588\t    end\n589\t\n590\t    test \"a 4th Logs child \\\"Trace Map\\\" (key logs-trace-map, /admin/logs/trace-map) exists alongside the 3 AC7 stubs\" do\n591\t      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n592\t\n593\t      logs_children = find(html, \"#nav-logs-children\")\n594\t      nested_keys = logs_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n595\t\n596\t      assert \"logs-trace-map\" in nested_keys,\n597\t             \"Design Update: expected an additional \\\"Trace Map\\\" nav item under Logs \" <>\n598\t               \"(key logs-trace-map) — the page itself is a later story, only the nav item \" <>\n599\t               \"is in scope here\"\n600\t\n601\t      # The 3 original AC7 stubs must still be present — the addendum is additive.\n602\t      for key <- [\"logs-monitoring\", \"logs-config\", \"logs-war-room\"] do\n603\t        assert key in nested_keys,\n604\t               \"Design Update must not remove any of AC7's original 3 stub children (#{key} missing)\"\n605\t      end\n606\t\n607\t      trace_map_link = find(logs_children, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-trace-map\"]))\n608\t      assert attr(trace_map_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n609\t    end\n610\t  end\n611\t\n612\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n613\t  # Acceptance Test 9 [AC11] — full-layout integration\n614\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n615\t\n616\t  describe \"Acceptance Test 9 [AC11]: data-navkey preserved on all items, including nested children\" do\n617\t    setup do\n618\t      install_bootstrap_completed()\n619\t      :ok\n620\t    end\n621\t\n622\t    test \"every nav item (parent and child) has a data-navkey attribute matching its key\" do\n623\t      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n624\t\n625\t      for key <- [\"dashboard\", \"compliance\", \"logs\", \"users\", \"rooms\", \"spaces\", \"config\"] do\n626\t        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n627\t               \"expected a top-level nav item with data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"\"\n628\t      end\n629\t\n630\t      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n631\t        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n632\t               \"AC11: [data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"] must still resolve after the item moves under a parent\"\n633\t      end\n634\t\n635\t      for key <- [\"logs-monitoring\", \"logs-config\", \"logs-war-room\", \"logs-trace-map\"] do\n636\t        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n637\t               \"AC11: [data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"] must resolve for the new Logs child stubs\"\n638\t      end\n639\t    end\n640\t  end\n641\tend\n642\t"}]},"uuid":"df5bb6b1-901d-45fc-a6fa-ff7f3bf6bfa2","timestamp":"2026-07-07T11:10:30.060Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_nested_nav_test.exs","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LayoutsNestedNavTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting`\n  (Acceptance Tests 1-11 and 13 from the story's \"Acceptance Tests\" section;\n  Acceptance Test 12 — zero-regression of the existing Playwright/BDD E2E\n  suite under the refactored nav — is a genuinely browser-only proof and\n  lives in `e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature` +\n  `e2e/step-definitions/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.steps.ts` instead. That\n  file also carries the real-keyboard half of AC9/AC10 (Tab/Space/Enter on\n  an actual focused DOM node — not reproducible against\n  `Phoenix.LiveViewTest`'s server-rendered HTML string). This mirrors the\n  codebase's established split between component-level ExUnit coverage and\n  real-browser Playwright coverage for the sibling `trace-map-hierarchy`\n  story (see `logs_live/trace_map_tree_test.exs`'s own moduledoc for the\n  identical pattern, and `logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature`'s header comment\n  for the browser-only half).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting.md\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) is\n  currently a PRIVATE (`defp`) 3-attr leaf-only component with no\n  `:children`/`:expanded` attr and no parent-with-toggle rendering mode at\n  all. Every `render_component(&Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1, ...)` call\n  below fails with `** (UndefinedFunctionError) function\n  Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1 is undefined or private` — calling a private\n  function via a remote capture is a compile-time-valid, runtime-failing\n  reference (mirrors this codebase's own `trace_map_tree_test.exs`\n  precedent for a target function that doesn't exist at all yet). The two\n  integration `describe` blocks at the bottom fail for a different, equally\n  real reason: `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` still renders \"Role Mapping\" /\n  \"Claim Mapping\" / \"Configuration\" as three flat sibling `<li>` items and\n  has no \"Logs\" parent with child stubs at all.\n\n  ## Component contract this file proposes (for the dev agent to implement —\n  concretizes the story's Dev Notes \"File Structure\" section, which only\n  sketches an example)\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` becomes PUBLIC and gains two new attrs on\n  top of the three (`:navigate`, `:key`, `:active`) that already exist:\n\n    * `:children` — list of `%{navigate: String.t(), key: String.t(),\n      label: String.t()}` maps, default `[]`. Empty (the default) renders\n      EXACTLY today's leaf markup (`<li><a data-navkey=... aria-current=...\n      class=...>label</a></li>`) — zero behavior change for every existing\n      call site that does not pass `:children` (AC2's \"a leaf item ...\n      renders exactly as before\"). This plain-map shape (rather than the\n      Dev Notes' sketched `:child` slot) mirrors the sibling\n      `TraceMapTree.endpoint_tree/1` component's own precedent of plain\n      data attrs over slots for a repeated child structure — the dev agent\n      may implement it with a slot instead IF the resulting HTML satisfies\n      every assertion below unchanged.\n    * `:expanded` — boolean, default `true` (Dev Notes Q3's recommended\n      \"all expanded by default\"). This file only pins down the RENDER\n      contract for a given `:expanded` value; what `live/1` computes that\n      value FROM (Dev Notes item 2's `parent_is_active?/3` sketch) is the\n      dev agent's wiring choice, exercised instead by the integration\n      `describe` blocks below via real `active_nav` values already assigned\n      by unrelated, unchanged controllers.\n\n  When `:children` is non-empty, `nav_item/1` renders a PARENT:\n\n    * A `<button type=\"button\" data-navkey={key} aria-expanded={to_string(\n      expanded)} aria-controls={\"nav-\\#{key}-children\"}>` (NOT an `<a>` —\n      AC9), containing a toggle-icon element (`data-testid=\n      \"nav-toggle-icon\"`, AC13) followed by the slot content (the parent's\n      own label).\n    * Toggle-icon element carries a class that differs between expanded and\n      collapsed (AC13's \"rotates/changes when expanded/collapsed\") — this\n      file pins down `\"rotate-90\"` present in the icon's class list when\n      `expanded: true`, absent when `expanded: false`, as the concrete,\n      testable proxy for that rotation (a CSS `transition` on that class\n      swap is an implementation detail this file does not test).\n    * Highlight classes on that `<button>`, in priority order (AC3/AC4):\n        1. `key == active` (the parent itself is the active nav) → the\n           SAME primary classes leaf items use today (`bg-primary\n           text-primary-content font-semibold`).\n        2. `active` matches one of `children`'s `:key`s (a child is active)\n           → a SECONDARY/accent class, `bg-base-300` (already used\n           elsewhere in this file for hover state — AC14's \"no new color\n           classes\"), explicitly NOT `bg-primary`.\n        3. otherwise (active belongs to neither this parent nor any of its\n           children — e.g. it names a key in a wholly different section)\n           → the same neutral classes leaf items use today. This is the\n           render-contract half of Acceptance Test 13.\n    * A sibling `<ul id={\"nav-\\#{key}-children\"} role=\"list\" class=\"ml-4 ...\">`\n      (AC12's indentation) carrying the boolean `hidden` attribute whenever\n      `expanded` is `false` (AC10 — children stay in the DOM either way,\n      Floki can always find them; only the `hidden` attribute toggles).\n      Each child renders `<li><a navigate={child.navigate}\n      data-navkey={child.key} aria-current={if child.key == active, do:\n      \"page\"} class={...}>{child.label}</a></li>` — primary classes when\n      `child.key == active`, neutral otherwise (AC4's \"other children ...\n      neutral\").\n\n  `live/1` itself must additionally: (a) reorganize \"Configuration\" into a\n  parent with `role-mapping`/`claim-mapping` children (AC5/AC6, removing\n  the two flat top-level items), (b) introduce a new \"Logs\" parent with\n  child stubs `logs-monitoring` (label \"Monitoring\", already-shipped\n  `/admin/logs`), `logs-config` (label \"Configuration\", `/admin/logs/config`)\n  and `logs-war-room` (label \"War Room\", `/admin/logs/war-room`) per\n  AC7/AC8, PLUS — per the story's later \"Design Update (2026-07-07)\"\n  addendum — a 4th child `logs-trace-map` (label \"Trace Map\",\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map`, the not-yet-built page from the Leitstand spec).\n  The addendum's wording (\"gains a third child 'Trace Map'\") does not\n  literally square with AC7's own enumeration of 3 stubs; per this\n  pipeline run's explicit instruction that \"the story is authoritative\n  where it and the spec differ in scope,\" this file treats the addendum as\n  ADDITIVE to AC7 (4 children total) rather than replacing any of AC7's 3\n  named stubs. See the dedicated describe block below and this run's ATDD\n  report for the full reasoning — a deviation worth the dev/reviewer's\n  explicit attention.\n\n  ## Auth / rendering strategy for the two integration `describe` blocks\n\n  Mirrors `config_controller_test.exs`'s `build_admin_conn/2` +\n  `Nebu.Admin.Endpoint.call/2` pattern EXACTLY. A full router-dispatched\n  request is required here (unlike `live_isolated/3` elsewhere in this test\n  suite) because `live_isolated/3` explicitly skips both the root AND the\n  `:live` layout — it would never render the sidebar at all. Real GETs to\n  the already-shipped `/admin/config` and `/admin/config/role-mapping`\n  controller routes exercise the REAL, production `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`\n  nav list and the REAL `active_nav` values `RoleMappingController` already\n  assigns (`\"role-mapping\"`) — nothing about those two controllers changes\n  in this story, only how the shared layout renders their (unchanged)\n  `active_nav`.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Plug.Test\n  import Plug.Conn\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest, only: [render_component: 2]\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.Layouts\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-admin-sidebar-nesting-test-key\", 2)\n\n  # ── component-level helpers (mirrors trace_map_tree_test.exs) ──────────\n\n  defp parsed(html), do: Floki.parse_fragment!(html)\n\n  defp render_nav_item(assigns) do\n    render_component(&Layouts.nav_item/1, assigns) |> parsed()\n  end\n\n  @config_children [\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/role-mapping\", key: \"role-mapping\", label: \"Role Mapping\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/claim-mapping\", key: \"claim-mapping\", label: \"Claim Mapping\"}\n  ]\n\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp find_all(html, selector), do: Floki.find(html, selector)\n  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n\n  defp class_includes?(node, class_name) do\n    case attr(node, \"class\") do\n      nil -> false\n      class -> class |> String.split() |> Enum.member?(class_name)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp hidden?(node), do: attr(node, \"hidden\") != nil\n\n  # ── integration-level helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp build_admin_conn(method, path) do\n    %{conn(method, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n    |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n    |> fetch_session()\n    |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n      \"admin\" => true,\n      \"sub\" => \"admin-1\",\n      \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n    })\n  end\n\n  defp endpoint_call(conn), do: @endpoint.call(conn, @endpoint.init([]))\n\n  defp install_bootstrap_completed do\n    Application.put_env(:nebu_http, :admin_bootstrap_store, fn\n      :bootstrap_completed?, [] -> {:ok, true}\n      _op, _args -> :default\n    end)\n\n    on_exit(fn -> Application.delete_env(:nebu_http, :admin_bootstrap_store) end)\n  end\n\n  defp get_page_html(path) do\n    conn = build_admin_conn(:get, path) |> endpoint_call()\n    assert conn.status == 200, \"expected 200 rendering #{path}, got #{conn.status}\"\n    Floki.parse_document!(conn.resp_body)\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 1 [AC1-AC2]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 1 [AC1-AC2]: nested nav items render with expand/collapse toggle\" do\n    test \"a parent item with children renders a toggle icon and a nested <ul> of children\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      assert find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"])),\n             \"AC1: expected a toggle/chevron icon inside the parent button\"\n\n      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n      assert children_ul, \"AC1: expected a nested <ul id=\\\"nav-config-children\\\">\"\n\n      assert length(find_all(children_ul, \"li > a\")) == 2,\n             \"AC1: expected exactly 2 rendered children\"\n    end\n\n    test \"a leaf item (no children) renders exactly as before — no toggle icon, plain <a>, no <button>\" do\n      html = render_nav_item(%{navigate: \"/admin/dashboard\", key: \"dashboard\", active: nil})\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"])),\n             \"AC1: a leaf item must NOT render a toggle icon\"\n\n      assert find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"dashboard\"])),\n             \"AC2: leaf item must render as a plain <a data-navkey=\\\"dashboard\\\">\"\n\n      refute find(html, \"button\"), \"AC2: leaf item must not render a <button>\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 2 [AC3-AC4]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 2 [AC3-AC4]: active-nav extends to parent when a child is active\" do\n    test \"child active: child gets primary highlight, parent gets secondary, other children neutral\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: \"role-mapping\",\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      parent_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert parent_button, \"expected the Configuration parent to render as a <button>\"\n\n      assert class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-base-300\"),\n             \"AC4: parent must get the secondary/accent highlight when a child is active\"\n      refute class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-primary\"),\n             \"AC4: parent must NEVER get the full primary highlight, even when a child is active\"\n\n      role_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]))\n      assert class_includes?(role_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\"),\n             \"AC4: the active child must get the full primary highlight\"\n      assert attr(role_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n\n      claim_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"claim-mapping\"]))\n      refute class_includes?(claim_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\"),\n             \"AC4: other (inactive) children must be neutral\"\n\n      # Test-review MINOR-2: aria-current=\"\" is INVALID ARIA — on an inactive\n      # item the attribute must be entirely ABSENT. (Phoenix's\n      # `aria-current={if cond, do: \"page\"}` idiom omits nil attribute values,\n      # so `nil` is the only correct rendered outcome here.)\n      assert attr(claim_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == nil,\n             \"AC4: an inactive child must not carry aria-current at all (\\\"\\\" is invalid ARIA)\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 3 [AC3]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 3 [AC3]: parent itself active\" do\n    test \"parent gets primary highlight, all children remain neutral\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: \"config\",\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      parent_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert class_includes?(parent_button, \"bg-primary\"),\n             \"AC3: the parent itself must get the primary highlight when it is the active nav\"\n\n      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n        link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"#{key}\"]))\n        refute class_includes?(link, \"bg-primary\"),\n               \"AC3: child #{key} must be neutral while the parent itself is active\"\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 6 [AC9]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 6 [AC9]: parent items render as buttons with aria-expanded\" do\n    test \"aria-expanded is \\\"true\\\" when expanded: true\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: true\n        })\n\n      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert button, \"AC9: parent must render as a <button>, not an <a>\"\n      assert attr(button, \"aria-expanded\") == \"true\"\n    end\n\n    test \"aria-expanded is \\\"false\\\" when expanded: false\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: false\n        })\n\n      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert attr(button, \"aria-expanded\") == \"false\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 7 [AC9]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 7 [AC9]: aria-controls establishes relationship to the child list\" do\n    test \"button's aria-controls matches the child <ul>'s id\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert attr(button, \"aria-controls\") == \"nav-config-children\"\n\n      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n      assert children_ul, \"expected a <ul id=\\\"nav-config-children\\\"> matching aria-controls\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 8 [AC10-AC11] (render-contract half — see moduledoc for\n  # the real-keyboard half in the Playwright feature file)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 8 [AC10]: collapsed children remain present in the DOM\" do\n    test \"when expanded: false, child <li>/<a> elements are still found by Floki (not removed), only `hidden`\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: false\n        })\n\n      children_ul = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n      assert hidden?(children_ul),\n             \"AC10: the collapsed child list must carry the `hidden` attribute\"\n\n      assert find(children_ul, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"])),\n             \"AC10: children must remain in the DOM (not conditionally excluded) even when collapsed\"\n      assert find(children_ul, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"claim-mapping\"]))\n    end\n\n    test \"when expanded: true, the child list does not carry `hidden`\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: true\n        })\n\n      refute hidden?(find(html, \"#nav-config-children\"))\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 10 [AC12]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 10 [AC12]: nested children are visually indented\" do\n    test \"the child <ul> carries the ml-4 indentation class\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      assert class_includes?(find(html, \"#nav-config-children\"), \"ml-4\"),\n             \"AC12: expected the nested child <ul> to carry ml-4 (or equivalent) indentation\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 11 [AC13]\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 11 [AC13]: parent items show a toggle chevron that changes with state\" do\n    test \"the toggle icon's class differs between expanded and collapsed\" do\n      expanded_html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: true\n        })\n\n      collapsed_html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: nil,\n          children: @config_children,\n          expanded: false\n        })\n\n      expanded_icon = find(expanded_html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]))\n      collapsed_icon = find(collapsed_html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"]))\n\n      assert class_includes?(expanded_icon, \"rotate-90\"),\n             \"AC13: expected the toggle icon to carry a rotation class when expanded\"\n      refute class_includes?(collapsed_icon, \"rotate-90\"),\n             \"AC13: expected the toggle icon's rotation class to be absent when collapsed\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 13 [AC3-AC4] (component-level half — proves the\n  # highlight-priority logic correctly falls through to \"neutral\" when\n  # `active` names a key from an entirely different, unrelated section;\n  # the two real top-level sections never share render state in `live/1`\n  # either, so testing one `nav_item/1` instance in isolation IS the\n  # correct unit boundary for this invariant)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 13 [AC3-AC4]: parent has no highlight when active_nav belongs to a different section\" do\n    test \"Configuration gets neutral classes when active_nav is \\\"logs-monitoring\\\" (a Logs child, not its own)\" do\n      html =\n        render_nav_item(%{\n          navigate: \"/admin/config\",\n          key: \"config\",\n          active: \"logs-monitoring\",\n          children: @config_children\n        })\n\n      button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      refute class_includes?(button, \"bg-primary\"),\n             \"must not be primary-highlighted for an unrelated section's active key\"\n      refute class_includes?(button, \"bg-base-300\"),\n             \"must not be secondary-highlighted for an unrelated section's active key\"\n\n      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n        refute class_includes?(find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])), \"bg-primary\")\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 4 [AC5-AC6] — full-layout integration\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 4 [AC5-AC6]: Configuration nesting removes flat Role Mapping / Claim Mapping\" do\n    setup do\n      install_bootstrap_completed()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"Role Mapping / Claim Mapping are no longer top-level items, but ARE nested Configuration children\" do\n      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n\n      # Test-review MAJOR-1: Floki 0.38.4 silently treats a LEADING child\n      # combinator (`find_all(li_node, \"> a\")`) as a DESCENDANT selector,\n      # which would have made this list include correctly-NESTED children and\n      # fail the refutes below against a CORRECT implementation. Full-path\n      # child combinators ARE honored, so select the direct top-level items in\n      # one shot: only <a>/<button> elements that are DIRECT children of a\n      # top-level <li> (nested children live one level deeper, inside the\n      # parent's own <ul id=\"nav-*-children\"> > li > a — never matched here).\n      top_level_keys =\n        html\n        |> find_all(\n          ~s(nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > a) <>\n            ~s(, nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > button)\n        )\n        |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n\n      refute \"role-mapping\" in top_level_keys,\n             \"AC6: Role Mapping must be removed as a flat top-level item\"\n      refute \"claim-mapping\" in top_level_keys,\n             \"AC6: Claim Mapping must be removed as a flat top-level item\"\n      assert \"config\" in top_level_keys,\n             \"AC5: Configuration must remain as a single top-level item\"\n\n      config_children = find(html, \"#nav-config-children\")\n      assert config_children, \"AC5: expected a #nav-config-children nested list\"\n\n      nested_keys = config_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n\n      assert \"role-mapping\" in nested_keys,\n             \"AC6: Role Mapping must be visible nested under Configuration when expanded\"\n      assert \"claim-mapping\" in nested_keys,\n             \"AC6: Claim Mapping must be visible nested under Configuration when expanded\"\n    end\n\n    test \"real active_nav wiring: on /admin/config/role-mapping, Configuration is secondary-highlighted and Role Mapping is primary-highlighted\" do\n      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config/role-mapping\")\n\n      config_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"config\"]))\n      assert config_button, \"expected Configuration to render as a parent <button>\"\n      assert class_includes?(config_button, \"bg-base-300\")\n      refute class_includes?(config_button, \"bg-primary\")\n\n      role_mapping_link = find(html, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]))\n      assert class_includes?(role_mapping_link, \"bg-primary\")\n      assert attr(role_mapping_link, \"aria-current\") == \"page\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 5 [AC7-AC8] — full-layout integration\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 5 [AC7-AC8]: Logs section is created with child stubs\" do\n    setup do\n      install_bootstrap_completed()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"a Logs parent item is visible, and expanding it shows Monitoring / Configuration / War Room stubs\" do\n      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n\n      logs_button = find(html, ~s(button[data-navkey=\"logs\"]))\n      assert logs_button, \"AC7: expected a \\\"Logs\\\" parent item\"\n\n      logs_children = find(html, \"#nav-logs-children\")\n      assert logs_children, \"AC7: expected a #nav-logs-children nested list\"\n\n      nested_keys = logs_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n\n      assert \"logs-monitoring\" in nested_keys,\n             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"Monitoring\\\" stub with key logs-monitoring\"\n      assert \"logs-config\" in nested_keys,\n             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"Configuration\\\" stub with key logs-config\"\n      assert \"logs-war-room\" in nested_keys,\n             \"AC7-AC8: expected a \\\"War Room\\\" stub with key logs-war-room\"\n\n      for key <- nested_keys do\n        assert String.starts_with?(key, \"logs-\"),\n               \"AC8: every Logs child key must be namespaced with \\\"logs-\\\" to avoid collisions, got #{key}\"\n      end\n\n      # AC7: the child link actually points somewhere navigable (no dead href).\n      monitoring_link = find(logs_children, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"]))\n      assert attr(monitoring_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Design Update (2026-07-07, appended to the story) — Trace Map nav item\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Design Update: Logs section additionally includes a Trace Map child (additive to AC7's 3 stubs)\" do\n    setup do\n      install_bootstrap_completed()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"a 4th Logs child \\\"Trace Map\\\" (key logs-trace-map, /admin/logs/trace-map) exists alongside the 3 AC7 stubs\" do\n      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n\n      logs_children = find(html, \"#nav-logs-children\")\n      nested_keys = logs_children |> find_all(\"a\") |> Enum.map(&attr(&1, \"data-navkey\"))\n\n      assert \"logs-trace-map\" in nested_keys,\n             \"Design Update: expected an additional \\\"Trace Map\\\" nav item under Logs \" <>\n               \"(key logs-trace-map) — the page itself is a later story, only the nav item \" <>\n               \"is in scope here\"\n\n      # The 3 original AC7 stubs must still be present — the addendum is additive.\n      for key <- [\"logs-monitoring\", \"logs-config\", \"logs-war-room\"] do\n        assert key in nested_keys,\n               \"Design Update must not remove any of AC7's original 3 stub children (#{key} missing)\"\n      end\n\n      trace_map_link = find(logs_children, ~s(a[data-navkey=\"logs-trace-map\"]))\n      assert attr(trace_map_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Acceptance Test 9 [AC11] — full-layout integration\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Acceptance Test 9 [AC11]: data-navkey preserved on all items, including nested children\" do\n    setup do\n      install_bootstrap_completed()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"every nav item (parent and child) has a data-navkey attribute matching its key\" do\n      html = get_page_html(\"/admin/config\")\n\n      for key <- [\"dashboard\", \"compliance\", \"logs\", \"users\", \"rooms\", \"spaces\", \"config\"] do\n        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n               \"expected a top-level nav item with data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"\"\n      end\n\n      for key <- [\"role-mapping\", \"claim-mapping\"] do\n        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n               \"AC11: [data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"] must still resolve after the item moves under a parent\"\n      end\n\n      for key <- [\"logs-monitoring\", \"logs-config\", \"logs-war-room\", \"logs-trace-map\"] do\n        assert find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"#{key}\"])),\n               \"AC11: [data-navkey=\\\"#{key}\\\"] must resolve for the new Logs child stubs\"\n      end\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":642,"startLine":1,"totalLines":642}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"64010607-59f1-44db-8bdc-6c1cf8425da9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"21a6f546-9b4c-48dd-ad69-e0cac0d3b519","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"90d26025-2b2c-439e-8eaf-4677446dbd12","agentId":"a65e9efd17f8c0889","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019HFTB5mTacpA7g23jyrucj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tFeature: Admin UI — Sidebar Nesting: collapsible parent sections for Configuration and Logs\n2\t  As a Nebu platform operator\n3\t  I want the admin sidebar to group related pages under collapsible parent sections\n4\t  So that the navigation stays uncluttered and the current section is always obvious\n5\t\n6\t  # ATDD, written FIRST (RED phase) — story: enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting\n7\t  # docs/stories/enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting.md\n8\t  #\n9\t  # Component-level coverage of this story's own Acceptance Tests 1-11 and 13\n10\t  # (parent/child render contract, aria-expanded/aria-controls markup,\n11\t  # highlight-priority logic, data-navkey preservation, Configuration/Logs\n12\t  # reorganization) lives in server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_nested_nav_test.exs\n13\t  # — see that file's moduledoc for the full component + integration\n14\t  # contract this story's dev-story pass must implement.\n15\t  #\n16\t  # This file covers the genuinely browser-only half Phoenix.LiveViewTest/Floki\n17\t  # cannot exercise: real keyboard focus + Space/Enter toggling a native\n18\t  # <button>'s aria-expanded state (AC9/AC10), and end-to-end proof that the\n19\t  # pre-existing E2E-relevant selectors/assertions this refactor must not\n20\t  # regress (data-navkey, aria-current=\"page\" — AC15/AC16) still resolve\n21\t  # against the REAL rendered page after the nesting refactor lands. Mirrors\n22\t  # the established split in this codebase between\n23\t  # logs_live/trace_map_tree_test.exs (component) and\n24\t  # logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature (real browser) for the sibling\n25\t  # trace-map-hierarchy story.\n26\t  #\n27\t  # NOTE on the legacy e2e/tests/tests/features/admin/role-mapping.spec.ts and\n28\t  # ux-polish-9-13.spec.ts referenced by AC15/AC16: those two files are\n29\t  # pre-migration Playwright specs NOT wired into playwright.config.ts's\n30\t  # default project run (see that config's own header comment — only\n31\t  # features/**/*.feature via playwright-bdd runs by default; the legacy\n32\t  # e2e/tests/ specs run only via `tests/playwright.legacy.config.ts`, run\n33\t  # manually). They are therefore not a live regression gate today. This\n34\t  # feature file's \"existing selectors still work\" scenario below is the\n35\t  # ACTIVE regression proof for AC15/AC16 in the currently-running suite,\n36\t  # written in this codebase's current BDD convention rather than resurrecting\n37\t  # the legacy plain-spec pattern.\n38\t  #\n39\t  # The @pending-feature scaffold tags were removed by the dev-story pass once\n40\t  # the nested nav markup landed (`Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` public with\n41\t  # parent/child rendering, aria-expanded/aria-controls, and the\n42\t  # Configuration/Logs reorganization) — these scenarios now run in the\n43\t  # default Playwright suite.\n44\t\n45\t  Background:\n46\t    Given the Nebu stack is running\n47\t    And bootstrap has been completed\n48\t    And the operator is logged in as admin\n49\t\n50\t  # Test-review MINOR-1: focus is reached via REAL repeated Tab keypresses\n51\t  # (not programmatic .focus()), and after re-expanding, one more Tab must\n52\t  # land INSIDE the expanded children (story Acceptance Test 8's \"child items\n53\t  # become visible and focusable via subsequent Tab presses\").\n54\t  # Test-review MINOR-3: the toggle must also resolve via the accessibility\n55\t  # tree as a real button with its visible label as its accessible name.\n56\t  @ac9-keyboard-nav @ac10-dom-presence\n57\t  Scenario: Keyboard-only Tab, then Space, toggles the Configuration nav section's aria-expanded state\n58\t    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config\"\n59\t    And the operator presses Tab until the \"config\" nav toggle is focused\n60\t    Then the \"config\" nav toggle resolves as an accessible button named \"Configuration\"\n61\t    And the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n62\t    When the operator presses Space on the focused nav toggle\n63\t    Then the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"false\"\n64\t    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is present in the DOM\n65\t    When the operator presses Space on the focused nav toggle\n66\t    Then the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n67\t    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is visible\n68\t    When the operator presses Tab once\n69\t    Then the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is focused\n70\t\n71\t  @ac9-keyboard-nav\n72\t  Scenario: Enter also toggles a nav section's aria-expanded state\n73\t    When the operator
