operator presses Enter on the focused nav toggle\n77\t    Then the \"logs\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"false\"\n78\t\n79\t  @ac15-regression @ac16-regression\n80\t  Scenario: Existing nav selectors and aria-current assertions still resolve after the nesting refactor\n81\t    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config/role-mapping\"\n82\t    Then the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is visible\n83\t    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" has aria-current \"page\"\n84\t    And the sidebar nav item \"config\" is visible\n85\t\n86\t  # Test-review MAJOR-2 rewrite: sections render EXPANDED by default (the\n87\t  # pinned `:expanded` default plus the previous scenario's initial\n88\t  # aria-expanded=\"true\"), so no toggle click is needed — clicking the toggle\n89\t  # first would COLLAPSE the section and make these visibility assertions\n90\t  # fail against a correct implementation. Starting page is /admin/config\n91\t  # (a non-logs page), so the final EXACT-path assertion cannot be satisfied\n92\t  # vacuously by the starting URL or by a sibling route like\n93\t  # /admin/logs/config.\n94\t  @ac7-logs-stub-navigation\n95\t  Scenario: The Logs section's child stubs are visible by default and the Monitoring stub navigates to /admin/logs\n96\t    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config\"\n97\t    Then the \"logs\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n98\t    And the sidebar nav item \"logs-monitoring\" is visible\n99\t    And the sidebar nav item \"logs-config\" is visible\n100\t    And the sidebar nav item \"logs-war-room\" is visible\n101\t    And the sidebar nav item \"logs-trace-map\" is visible\n102\t    When the operator clicks the sidebar nav item \"logs-monitoring\"\n103\t    Then the page URL path is exactly \"/admin/logs\"\n104\t"}]},"uuid":"0e36bce2-a337-4da9-9f16-227220e1a60a","timestamp":"2026-07-07T11:10:40.196Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature","content":"Feature: Admin UI — Sidebar Nesting: collapsible parent sections for Configuration and Logs\n  As a Nebu platform operator\n  I want the admin sidebar to group related pages under collapsible parent sections\n  So that the navigation stays uncluttered and the current section is always obvious\n\n  # ATDD, written FIRST (RED phase) — story: enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting\n  # docs/stories/enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting.md\n  #\n  # Component-level coverage of this story's own Acceptance Tests 1-11 and 13\n  # (parent/child render contract, aria-expanded/aria-controls markup,\n  # highlight-priority logic, data-navkey preservation, Configuration/Logs\n  # reorganization) lives in server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_nested_nav_test.exs\n  # — see that file's moduledoc for the full component + integration\n  # contract this story's dev-story pass must implement.\n  #\n  # This file covers the genuinely browser-only half Phoenix.LiveViewTest/Floki\n  # cannot exercise: real keyboard focus + Space/Enter toggling a native\n  # <button>'s aria-expanded state (AC9/AC10), and end-to-end proof that the\n  # pre-existing E2E-relevant selectors/assertions this refactor must not\n  # regress (data-navkey, aria-current=\"page\" — AC15/AC16) still resolve\n  # against the REAL rendered page after the nesting refactor lands. Mirrors\n  # the established split in this codebase between\n  # logs_live/trace_map_tree_test.exs (component) and\n  # logs-trace-map-hierarchy.feature (real browser) for the sibling\n  # trace-map-hierarchy story.\n  #\n  # NOTE on the legacy e2e/tests/tests/features/admin/role-mapping.spec.ts and\n  # ux-polish-9-13.spec.ts referenced by AC15/AC16: those two files are\n  # pre-migration Playwright specs NOT wired into playwright.config.ts's\n  # default project run (see that config's own header comment — only\n  # features/**/*.feature via playwright-bdd runs by default; the legacy\n  # e2e/tests/ specs run only via `tests/playwright.legacy.config.ts`, run\n  # manually). They are therefore not a live regression gate today. This\n  # feature file's \"existing selectors still work\" scenario below is the\n  # ACTIVE regression proof for AC15/AC16 in the currently-running suite,\n  # written in this codebase's current BDD convention rather than resurrecting\n  # the legacy plain-spec pattern.\n  #\n  # The @pending-feature scaffold tags were removed by the dev-story pass once\n  # the nested nav markup landed (`Nebu.Admin.Layouts.nav_item/1` public with\n  # parent/child rendering, aria-expanded/aria-controls, and the\n  # Configuration/Logs reorganization) — these scenarios now run in the\n  # default Playwright suite.\n\n  Background:\n    Given the Nebu stack is running\n    And bootstrap has been completed\n    And the operator is logged in as admin\n\n  # Test-review MINOR-1: focus is reached via REAL repeated Tab keypresses\n  # (not programmatic .focus()), and after re-expanding, one more Tab must\n  # land INSIDE the expanded children (story Acceptance Test 8's \"child items\n  # become visible and focusable via subsequent Tab presses\").\n  # Test-review MINOR-3: the toggle must also resolve via the accessibility\n  # tree as a real button with its visible label as its accessible name.\n  @ac9-keyboard-nav @ac10-dom-presence\n  Scenario: Keyboard-only Tab, then Space, toggles the Configuration nav section's aria-expanded state\n    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config\"\n    And the operator presses Tab until the \"config\" nav toggle is focused\n    Then the \"config\" nav toggle resolves as an accessible button named \"Configuration\"\n    And the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n    When the operator presses Space on the focused nav toggle\n    Then the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"false\"\n    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is present in the DOM\n    When the operator presses Space on the focused nav toggle\n    Then the \"config\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is visible\n    When the operator presses Tab once\n    Then the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is focused\n\n  @ac9-keyboard-nav\n  Scenario: Enter also toggles a nav section's aria-expanded state\n    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config\"\n    And the operator presses Tab until the \"logs\" nav toggle is focused\n    Then the \"logs\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"true\"\n    When the operator presses Enter on the focused nav toggle\n    Then the \"logs\" nav toggle has aria-expanded \"false\"\n\n  @ac15-regression @ac16-regression\n  Scenario: Existing nav selectors and aria-current assertions still resolve after the nesting refactor\n    When the operator navigates to \"/admin/config/role-mapping\"\n    Then the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" is visible\n    And the sidebar nav item \"role-mapping\" has aria-current \"page\"\n    And the sidebar nav item \"config\" is visible\n\n  # first would COLLAPSE the section and make these visibility assertions\n  # fail against a correct implementation. Parent nav toggle: a native `<button data-navkey={key}\n *     aria-expanded=\"true\"|\"false\" aria-controls=\"nav-{key}-children\">`.\n *   - Nav item (parent OR child): `[data-navkey={key}]` — a `<button>` for\n *     parents, an `<a>` for leaves/children. Mirrors logs-trace-map-hierarchy.steps.ts's\n// \"repeatedly pressing Tab reaches ...\" precedent. 40 presses is a generous\n// ceiling: skip-link + topbar + all top-level items + both sections'\n// default-expanded children total well under that.\nWhen(\n  'the operator presses Tab until the {string} nav toggle is focused',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    const toggle = navItemLocator(page, key);\n    let focused = false;\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {\n      await page.keyboard.press('Tab');\n\n      if (await toggle.evaluate((el) => el === document.activeElement)) {\n        focused = true;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    expect(focused, `Tab traversal never reached [data-navkey=\"${key}\"]`).toBe(true);\n  },\n);\n\n// operator presses Tab once', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await page.keyboard.press('Tab');\n});\n\nWhen(\n  'the operator clicks the {string} nav toggle',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    await navItemLocator(page, key).click();\n  },\n);\n\n// AC9: proves the disclosure toggle works via keyboard alone (Space on a\n// focused native <button>), not merely via `.click()` (which Playwright can\n// dispatch even against elements a real keyboard-only user could never\n// reach/activate the same way).\nWhen('the operator presses Space on the focused nav toggle', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await page.keyboard.press('Space');\n});\n\nWhen('the operator presses Enter on the focused nav toggle', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');\n});\n\nWhen(\n  'the operator clicks the sidebar nav item {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    await navItemLocator(page, key).click();\n  },\n);\n\n// ─── Then: aria-expanded state ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nThen(\n  'the {string} nav toggle has aria-expanded {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string, expected: string) => {\n    await expect(navItemLocator(page, key)).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', expected, {\n      timeout: 10_000,\n    });\n  },\n);\n\n// Test-review MINOR-3 (AC9): the toggle must be exposed through the REAL\n// accessibility tree as a button whose accessible name is its visible label —\n// getByRole would not resolve a click-wired <div>, or a <button> whose label\n// is hidden from AT. Intersecting with the data-navkey locator proves both\n// resolve to the SAME element (data-navkey stays the primary selector\n// everywhere else, per AC11).\nThen(\n  'the {string} nav toggle resolves as an accessible button named {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string, name: string) => {\n    const byRole = page.getByRole('button', { name, exact: true });\n    await expect(byRole.and(navItemLocator(page, key))).toHaveCount(1, { timeout: 10_000 });\n  },\n);\n\n// ─── Then: sidebar item visibility / DOM presence / focus / active state ────\n\nThen(\n  'the sidebar nav item {string} is visible',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    await expect(navItemLocator(page, key)).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n  },\n);\n\n// AC10: a collapsed child must still be reachable in the DOM (not removed by\n// conditional rendering) even while visually hidden — deliberately does NOT\n// assert visibility here, only DOM attachment.\nThen(\n  'the sidebar nav item {string} is present in the DOM',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    await expect(navItemLocator(page, key)).toHaveCount(1, { timeout: 10_000 });\n  },\n);\n\n// Test-review MINOR-1 (story Acceptance Test 8): proves the child became\n// genuinely focusable and is the very next Tab stop after its expanded parent.\nThen(\n  'the sidebar nav item {string} is focused',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string) => {\n    const isFocused = await navItemLocator(page, key).evaluate(\n      (el) => el === document.activeElement,\n    );\n    expect(isFocused, `[data-navkey=\"${key}\"] should be the active element`).toBe(true);\n  },\n);\n\nThen(\n  'the sidebar nav item {string} has aria-current {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, key: string, expected: string) => {\n    await expect(navItemLocator(page, key)).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', expected, {\n      timeout: 10_000,\n    });\n  },\n);\n\n// Test-review MAJOR-2: EXACT pathname comparison — a \"URL contains\" substring\n// check would be satisfied vacuously by sibling routes (/admin/logs/config,\n// /admin/logs/war-room) or by a page that never navigated at all if the\n// starting URL already contained the substring.\nThen(\n  'the page URL path is exactly {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, path: string) => {\n    await page.waitForURL((url) {"parentUuid":"83e24999-7ca3-4c63-b07a-45a702be18f7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"90d26025-2b2c-439e-8eaf-4677446dbd12","agentId":"a65e9efd17f8c0889","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01L4ZxBYRA6eiNXxd2RQGY1A","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Test Review — admin-sidebar-nesting (RED-phase ATDD) — 2026-07-07\n2\t\n3\tVerdict: 2 MAJOR, 3 MINOR (request changes before dev-story)\n4\t\n5\t## MAJOR-1 — AC5/AC6 integration test cannot pass against a correct implementation (broken Floki selector)\n6\t`server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_nested_nav_test.exs:490` — `find_all(&1, \"> a, > button\")`:\n7\tFloki 0.38.4 silently treats a leading child combinator as a DESCENDANT selector, so\n8\t`top_level_keys` includes nested `role-mapping`/`claim-mapping` even when correctly nested;\n9\t`refute \"role-mapping\" in top_level_keys` then fails on the CORRECT implementation.\n10\tFix: full-path child-combinator selector, e.g.\n11\t`nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > a, ... > li > button`,\n12\tor walk the `{tag, attrs, children}` tuples manually.\n13\t\n14\t## MAJOR-2 — Gherkin scenario 4 contradicts the pinned default-expanded contract\n15\t`e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature:78-87` — clicks the \"logs\" toggle then\n16\texpects children to become VISIBLE, but the pinned contract (`:expanded` default true;\n17\tscenario 2 line 67 asserts initial aria-expanded=\"true\") means the first click COLLAPSES.\n18\tAlso the final `URL contains \"/admin/logs\"` assertion is vacuous. Fix: drop the toggle\n19\tclick (children visible by default) or collapse-then-expand explicitly; navigate from a\n20\tnon-logs page and assert the exact URL after clicking `logs-monitoring`.\n21\t\n22\t## MINOR-1 — Tab operability untested (AC9 / story Acceptance Test 8)\n23\t`e2e/step-definitions/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.steps.ts:67-72` uses programmatic\n24\t`.focus()` instead of real Tab keypresses; no Tab-into-expanded-children assertion.\n25\t\n26\t## MINOR-2 — Loose aria-current assertion\n27\t`layouts_nested_nav_test.exs:264` accepts `aria-current in [nil, \"\"]` — empty string is\n28\tinvalid ARIA; pin to `nil` (attribute absent).\n29\t\n30\t## MINOR-3 — Toggle steps never assert button role in the browser\n31\tAdd one `getByRole('button', { name: ... })` resolution in scenario 1 (data-navkey\n32\tlocating stays per AC11).\n33\t\n34\t## INFO\n35\t- No hard waits/non-determinism; async false + on_exit cleanup correct.\n36\t- GenServer crash test correctly absent (pure UI story).\n37\t- Patterns verified against codebase (build_admin_conn, :nebu_http seam, reused steps).\n38\t- AC coverage otherwise complete incl. Design-Update Trace-Map child (own describe block).\n39\t"}]},"uuid":"b53bcae0-04e0-4683-b8e2-4fe59298f0cf","timestamp":"2026-07-07T11:13:33.231Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/admin-sidebar-nesting/test-review-findings.md","content":"# Test Review — admin-sidebar-nesting (RED-phase ATDD) — 2026-07-07\n\nVerdict: 2 MAJOR, 3 MINOR (request changes before dev-story)\n\n## MAJOR-1 — AC5/AC6 integration test cannot pass against a correct implementation (broken Floki selector)\n`server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_nested_nav_test.exs:490` — `find_all(&1, \"> a, > button\")`:\nFloki 0.38.4 silently treats a leading child combinator as a DESCENDANT selector, so\n`top_level_keys` includes nested `role-mapping`/`claim-mapping` even when correctly nested;\n`refute \"role-mapping\" in top_level_keys` then fails on the CORRECT implementation.\nFix: full-path child-combinator selector, e.g.\n`nav[aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"] > ul[role=\"list\"] > li > a, ... > li > button`,\nor walk the `{tag, attrs, children}` tuples manually.\n\n## MAJOR-2 — Gherkin scenario 4 contradicts the pinned default-expanded contract\n`e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature:78-87` — clicks the \"logs\" toggle then\nexpects children to become VISIBLE, but the pinned contract (`:expanded` default true;\nscenario 2 line 67 asserts initial aria-expanded=\"true\") means the first click COLLAPSES.\nAlso the final `URL contains \"/admin/logs\"` assertion is vacuous. {"parentUuid":"f145741e-4481-48f6-aec7-d255837608ad","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a65e9efd17f8c0889","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CcndAP2PtQt4ohnrYsdyK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"0 MAJOR, 3 MINOR, 5 INFO\n\n# Code Review — admin-sidebar-nesting (enhanced-logging), cycle 0\n\n**Scope reviewed:** full staged diff — `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`, `app.js`, `router.ex`, `config_controller.ex`, `logs_live.ex`, `logs_controller.ex`, `layouts_nested_nav_test.exs`, `admin-sidebar-nesting.feature` + steps, `e2e/fixtures/dex-preflight.ts`, rebuilt `app.css`.\n\n## AC coverage verdict\n\nAll 17 ACs + the Design Update addendum are implemented and test-covered:\n\n- **AC1/AC2** — dual-clause public `nav_item/1` (leaf clause byte-compatible with pre-nesting markup; parent clause disclosure group). Verified in diff + tests 1–2.\n- **AC3/AC4** — highlight priority (primary / `bg-base-300` secondary / neutral) correct in the `cond` at `layouts.ex`, covered by component tests 2, 3, 13 AND a real-request integration test against `/admin/config/role-mapping`.\n- **AC5/AC6** — flat Role/Claim Mapping removed from `live/1`, present only in `@config_nav_children`. Integration test uses the corrected full-path Floki child-combinator (test-review MAJOR-1 fix verified in place, `layouts_nested_nav_test.exs:501-508`).\n- **AC7/AC8** — Logs parent with namespaced child keys; stub routes `/logs/trace-map`, `/logs/war-room` → `LogsLive` so no child click errors. All `active_nav` write sites grepped: `role-mapping`/`claim-mapping` controllers unchanged and correct; `logs_live.ex` → `logs-monitoring`, `logs_controller.ex` → `logs-config`, `config_controller.ex` → `config-general` (UX gate V1).\n- **AC9/AC10** — `<button aria-expanded aria-controls>`, children always in DOM, only boolean `hidden` toggles. Keyboard half proven in browser via real Tab traversal + Space/Enter scenarios.\n- **AC11** — all `data-navkey` selectors preserved; `a[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]` (role-mapping.spec.ts) still matches an `<a>` with `aria-current=\"page\"`.\n- **AC12/AC13/AC14** — `ml-4` indent, chevron with `rotate-90`, only existing DaisyUI/Tailwind theme classes; all new utilities (`rotate-90`, `transition-transform`, `space-y-1`, `shrink-0`, `text-left`) verified present in the rebuilt `app.css`, and the load-bearing preflight rule `[hidden]:where(:not([hidden=until-found])){display:none}` is present with no display utility on the child `<ul>` to defeat it (the trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 cascade bug is correctly avoided).\n- **AC15/AC16** — see INFO-4.\n- **CSP/conventions** — no inline JS (delegated listener in `app.js`, wired once in `boot()`, survives morphdom, works on dead renders); no ADR-018/019/021 violations; no logging of anything; no node-local state (collapse state is per-tab DOM only, intentionally non-persistent per Q1).\n- **Pre-dev test-review findings** — all 5 (2 MAJOR, 3 MINOR) verifiably carried through into the final tests, each labeled at its fix site.\n\n## MINOR\n\n**MINOR-1 — Stale scaffold comment in the steps file contradicts current state.**\n`e2e/step-definitions/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.steps.ts:49-57` (\"Verification status of this scaffold\") still says the scenarios \"are tagged `@pending-feature` (excluded from the default Playwright run) ... until the dev-story implements the nested nav markup\" and \"were NOT executed against a live browser\". The tags were removed, the markup landed, and CI ran the 4 scenarios (4/4 PASS). The feature file's header was updated; this file was not. Update the comment to reflect post-implementation reality (same recurring pattern as previous cross-file comment staleness in this epic).\n\n**MINOR-2 — No browser assertion that collapsed children are actually *invisible*.**\n`e2e/features/admin/admin-sidebar-nesting.feature:63-64`: after Space-collapsing, the scenario asserts `role-mapping` \"is present in the DOM\" (AC10) but never asserts it is NOT visible while collapsed. Call sites must pass `navigate=\"/admin/config\"` / `\"/admin/logs\"` that do nothing, which misleads future callers into believing the parent navigates. Either make the attr optional with a doc note that it is leaf-only, or document the dead attr at the parent clause.
